>   local i1, i2, spr1, spr2;
>   if spread1 = spread2 then
>     return 0;
>   else
>     spr1 := spreadsort(spread1);
>     spr2 := spreadsort(spread2);
>     i2 := inv2(spr1, spr2);
>     if i2 = 3 then
>       return 6;
>     elif i2 = 1 then
>       return 5;
>     else
>       i1 := inv1(spr1,spr2);
>       if i2 = 2 then
>         if i1 = -2 then
>           return 3;
>         else
>           return 4;
>         fi;
>       else
>         if i1 = -2 then
>           return 2;
>         else
>           return 1;
>         fi;
>       fi;
>     fi;
>   fi;
> end;;
